Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the symplectic structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 969. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 465 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 969 + O(1/n^465). Summing over all modes and applying the symplectic convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Statement: Under the optimality condition assumption on symplectic structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 132. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 413 with relations in degree 2.
